Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int VS>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\nspmvCsrVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                    const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters, const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                    const index_t numRows, const index_t base, double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t row = tid / VS;\n    const unsigned lane = static_cast<unsigned>(tid) & (VS - 1);\n\n    double t = 0.0;\n    if (row < numRows) {\n        const index_t start = __ldg(rowDelimiters + row) - base;\n        const index_t end = __ldg(rowDelimiters + row + 1) - base;\n        // Align the start to a VS boundary for better coalescing\n        index_t j = (start & ~static_cast<index_t>(VS - 1)) + lane;\n        if (j >= start && j < end) {\n            t += __ldcs(val + j) * __ldg(vec + __ldcs(cols + j));\n        }\n        j += VS;\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (; j < end; j += VS) {\n            t += __ldcs(val + j) * __ldg(vec + __ldcs(cols + j));\n        }\n    }\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int offset = VS / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n        t += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset);\n    }\n    if (row < numRows && lane == 0) {\n        out[row] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-GPU slice of the CSR matrix\nstruct DeviceSlice {\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n    index_t rowBegin = 0, rowEnd = 0;  // rows [rowBegin, rowEnd)\n    index_t nnzBegin = 0, nnzEnd = 0;  // non-zeros [nnzBegin, nnzEnd)\n    double* d_val = nullptr;\n    index_t* d_cols = nullptr;\n    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;\n    double* d_vec = nullptr;\n    double* d_out = nullptr;\n};\n\ntemplate <int VS>\nstatic void launchSpmv(const DeviceSlice& s) {\n    const index_t rows = s.rowEnd - s.rowBegin;\n    if (rows == 0) return;\n    constexpr int threads = 256;\n    const size_t totalThreads = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * VS;\n    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((totalThreads + threads - 1) / threads);\n    spmvCsrVectorKernel<VS><<<blocks, threads, 0, s.stream>>>(\n        s.d_val, s.d_cols, s.d_rowDelimiters, s.d_vec, rows, s.nnzBegin, s.d_out);\n}\n\nstatic void launchSpmvVS(const DeviceSlice& s, int vs) {\n    switch (vs) {\n        case 1: launchSpmv<1>(s); break;\n        case 2: launchSpmv<2>(s); break;\n        case 4: launchSpmv<4>(s); break;\n        case 8: launchSpmv<8>(s); break;\n        case 16: launchSpmv<16>(s); break;\n        default: launchSpmv<32>(s); break;\n    }\n}\n''')\n\n# --- init in main\nsrc = src.replace('''    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n''', '''    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\\\n\");\n    if (fastRandMatchesLibc()) {\n        // rand() is unseeded at this point in the original, i.e. seed 1\n        GlibcRand rng(1);\n        fillWith(rng, h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fillWith(rng, h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrixFast(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n    } else {\n        srand(1);  // equivalent to the unseeded initial state\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n    }\n\n    // Set up the GPUs: partition rows so each device gets ~equal non-zeros\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount < 1) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device found\\\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    constexpr size_t MIN_NNZ_PER_DEVICE = size_t(1) << 22;\n    int numDevices = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(\n        static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount),\n        std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(nItems) / MIN_NNZ_PER_DEVICE)));\n    numDevices = std::max(1, std::min<int>(numDevices, std::max<index_t>(numRows, 1)));\n\n    // Threads per row: power of two near the average row length (max one warp)\n    const double avgRowLen = numRows ? static_cast<double>(nItems) / numRows : 0.0;\n    int vs = 1;\n    while (vs < 32 && vs * 2 <= avgRowLen) vs *= 2;\n\n    std::vector<DeviceSlice> slices(numDevices);\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDevices; ++d) {\n        DeviceSlice& s = slices[d];\n        s.device = d;\n        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(nItems) * d / numDevices;\n        s.rowBegin = (d == 0) ? 0\n            : static_cast<index_t>(std::lower_bound(h_rowDelimiters.begin(),\n                                                    h_rowDelimiters.begin() + numRows,\n                                                    static_cast<index_t>(target)) -\n                                   h_rowDelimiters.begin());\n    }\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDevices; ++d) {\n        DeviceSlice& s = slices[d];\n        s.rowEnd = (d + 1 < numDevices) ? slices[d + 1].rowBegin : numRows;\n        s.nnzBegin = h_rowDelimiters[s.rowBegin];\n        s.nnzEnd = h_rowDelimiters[s.rowEnd];\n        const index_t rows = s.rowEnd - s.rowBegin;\n        const size_t nnz = s.nnzEnd - s.nnzBegin;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&s.stream, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.d_out, std::max<size_t>(rows, 1) * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.d_val, h_val.data() + s.nnzBegin, nnz * sizeof(double),\n I changed only `spmv/spmv.cpp` and `spmv/CMakeLists.txt`, and the executable is still `spmv`.\n\nOn a 20000×20000 matrix with 1 in 10 entries non-zero, one SpMV iteration takes 0.15–0.18 ms on all 4 RTX 3090s, down from 62 ms on the CPU. On one GPU it takes 0.59 ms, which is about 815 GB/s, close to the card's memory bandwidth limit. Validation (`-v`) passed in every test run.\n\n**How the GPU version works:**\n- **Kernel:** a small group of threads (1–32) handles each matrix row and adds up its partial sums at the end. The group size is picked from the average number of non-zeros per row.\n- **Multiple GPUs:** for matrices with at least about 8M non-zeros (twice the 4M-per-GPU minimum), rows are split across the available GPUs so each gets roughly the same number of non-zeros. Each GPU computes its own part of the output, so they don't need to exchange data. 4 GPUs are about 3.3× faster than one, and smaller problems use a single GPU.\n- **Timed region:** it covers the kernel launches for all iterations plus copying the result back. Uploading the input to the GPUs happens before the timer starts, along with one untimed warm-up run.\n\n**Setup time:** with the original random-number calls, building the input took several seconds for large matrices. I made it faster without changing the generated data:\n- The code reproduces glibc's `rand()` sequence directly, which avoids the per-call overhead, and the matrix-filling loop is now branch-free. At startup it checks that its output matches the real `rand()`; if not, it falls back to the original code. The tested matrices and vectors came out the same as the original's.\n- GPU driver startup runs on a background thread while the input is generated, and data is uploaded to all GPUs in parallel.\n\n**Things to know:**\n- **Last-digit differences:** the GPU adds each row's values in a different order, so results differ from the original in the last digit or two. The `Sum` values match exactly, and the 2% validation tolerance easily covers this. The exact `Hash` in the `-r` output will not match the original.\n- **Remaining startup cost:** about 2–3 s per run is GPU driver initialization, because the GPUs aren't in persistence mode.
